PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#@#
###

'''
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

#@#
###

'''
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban-domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types
    location
    agent
    box
    direction
  )

  (:predicates
    (is_wall ?l - location)
    (is_goal ?l - location)
    (at_player ?p - agent ?l - location)
    (at_box ?b - box ?l - location)
    (adjacent ?l1 ?l2 - location ?d - direction)
  )

  ;; Player moves to an empty adjacent location
  (:action move_player_up
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_up)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_down
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_down)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_left
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_left)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_right
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_right)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  ;; Player pushes a box to an adjacent location
  (:action push_box_up
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_up) ; Player is below the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_up)   ; Box target is above the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next)) ; Ensure the box's target is empty
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)           ; Player moves to box's old position
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)              ; Box moves to new position
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_down
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_down) ; Player is above the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_down)   ; Box target is below the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_left
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_left) ; Player is right of the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_left)   ; Box target is left of the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_right
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_right) ; Player is left of the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_right)   ; Box target is right of the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)
    )
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>

</problem>
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban-domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types
    location
    agent
    box
    direction
  )

  (:predicates
    (is_wall ?l - location)
    (is_goal ?l - location)
    (at_player ?p - agent ?l - location)
    (at_box ?b - box ?l - location)
    (adjacent ?l1 ?l2 - location ?d - direction)
  )

  ;; Player moves to an empty adjacent location
  (:action move_player_up
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_up)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_down
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_down)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_left
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_left)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_right
    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_right)
      (not (is_wall ?to))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?from))
      (at_player ?p ?to)
    )
  )

  ;; Player pushes a box to an adjacent location
  (:action push_box_up
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_up) ; Player is below the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_up)   ; Box target is above the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next)) ; Ensure the box's target is empty
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)           ; Player moves to box's old position
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)              ; Box moves to new position
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_down
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_down) ; Player is above the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_down)   ; Box target is below the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_left
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_left) ; Player is right of the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_left)   ; Box target is left of the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_right
    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)
    :precondition (and
      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)
      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)
      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_right) ; Player is left of the box
      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_right)   ; Box target is right of the box
      (not (is_wall ?box_next))
      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))
      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))
      (at_box ?b ?box_next)
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain sokoban-domain)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n  (:types\n    location\n    agent\n    box\n    direction\n  )\n\n  (:predicates\n    (is_wall ?l - location)\n    (is_goal ?l - location)\n    (at_player ?p - agent ?l - location)\n    (at_box ?b - box ?l - location)\n    (adjacent ?l1 ?l2 - location ?d - direction)\n  )\n\n  ;; Player moves to an empty adjacent location\n  (:action move_player_up\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_up)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_player_down\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_down)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_player_left\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_left)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_player_right\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adjacent ?from ?to dir_right)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box box_1 ?to))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Player pushes a box to an adjacent location\n  (:action push_box_up\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)\n      (adjacent ?player_loc ?box_curr dir_up) ; Player is below the box\n      (adjacent ?box_curr ?box_next dir_up)   ; Box target is above the box\n      (not (is_wall ?box_next))\n      (not (at_box box_1 ?box_next)) ; Ensure the box's target is empty\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)           ;                 "You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.\nYour task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.\nAll provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.\nThe mapping is the following:\n'''\n@ - Player\n+ - Player on Goal\n$ - Box\n* - Box on Goal\n. - Goal ( Empty )\n# - Wall Brick\n'''\nThe game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.\nThe player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.\nWhen the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.\nAfter pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.\nThe player cannot pull the box, only push it.\nThe actions you can perform in the game are:\n'''\nL - Move Left\nR - Move Right\nU - Move Up\nD - Move Down\n'''\nAll provided problems CAN be solved.\nYou must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.\nYou must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.\nYou must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.\nThe following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:\nProblem:\n#####\n#@ ##\n## $ ##\n# #\n##. ##\n## #\n###\nSolution:\n<plan>\nR ,R ,D ,D\n</plan>\n\nHere is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:\n'''\n###\n#.#\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n#$#\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n#@#\n###\n\n'''",

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain sokoban-domain)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n  (:types\n    location\n    agent\n    box\n  )\n\n  (:predicates\n    (is_wall ?l - location)\n    (is_goal ?l - location)\n    (at_player ?p - agent ?l - location)\n    (at_box ?b - box ?l - location)\n    (connected_up ?l1 ?l2 - location)\n    (connected_down ?l1 ?l2 - location)\n    (connected_left ?l1 ?l2 - location)\n    (connected_right ?l1 ?l2 - location)\n  )\n\n  ;; Player moves to an empty adjacent location\n  (:action move_player_up\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (connected_up ?from ?to)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?to)) ; Assumes ?b is the only box.\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_player_down\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (connected_down ?from ?to)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?to)) ; Assumes ?b is the only box.\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_player_left\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (connected_left ?from ?to)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?to)) ; Assumes ?b is the only box.\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_player_right\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?from - location ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (connected_right ?from ?to)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?to)) ; Assumes ?b is the only box.\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Player pushes a box to an adjacent location\n  (:action push_box_up\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)\n      (connected_up ?player_loc ?box_curr) ; Player is immediately below the box (relative to direction of push)\n      (connected_up ?box_curr ?box_next)   ; Box target is immediately above the box\n      (not (is_wall ?box_next))\n      (not (at_player ?p ?box_next))       ; Box target cannot be occupied by the player\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_next))          ; Box target cannot contain the box itself (implicitly handled, but explicit)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)           ; Player moves to box's old position\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))\n      (at_box ?b ?box_next)              ; Box moves to new position\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_box_down\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)\n      (connected_down ?player_loc ?box_curr) ; Player is immediately above the box\n      (connected_down ?box_curr ?box_next)   ; Box target is immediately below the box\n      (not (is_wall ?box_next))\n      (not (at_player ?p ?box_next))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_next))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))\n      (at_box ?b ?box_next)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_box_left\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)\n      (connected_left ?player_loc ?box_curr) ; Player is immediately right of the box\n      (connected_left ?box_curr ?box_next)   ; Box target is immediately left of the box\n      (not (is_wall ?box_next))\n      (not (at_player ?p ?box_next))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_next))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_curr)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_curr))\n      (at_box ?b ?box_next)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_box_right\n    :parameters (?p - agent ?b - box ?player_loc - location ?box_curr - location ?box_next - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_curr)\n      (connected_right ?player_loc ?box_curr) ; '''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left

A location is free if no player and no box is on it\n    (adj_left ?from ?to - location)\n    (adj_right ?from ?to - location)\n    (adj_up ?from ?to - location)\n    (adj_down ?from ?to - location)\n  )\n\n  (:functions (total-cost))\n\n  ;; New box location becomes occupied\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_right\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_right ?player_loc ?box_loc)    ; Player pushing box actions\n  (:action push_left\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_right ?player_loc ?box_loc)    ; Player is to the right of the box, pushing it left\n      (adj_left ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)    ; Box moves left\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))        ; New box location must not be a wall\n      (not (at_player ?p ?new_box_loc))   ; New box location must not have player\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))      ; New box location must not have another box\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)             ; Player moves to old box location\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)            ; Box moves to new location\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_right\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_left ?player_loc ?box_loc)     ; Player is to the left of the box, pushing it right\n      (adj_right ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)   ; Box moves right\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_player ?p ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_up\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_down ?player_loc ?box_loc)     ; Player is below the box, pushing it up\n      (adj_up ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)      ; Box moves up\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_player ?p ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_down\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_up ?player_loc ?box_loc)       ; Player is above the box, pushing it down\n      (adj_down ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)    ; Box moves down\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_player ?p ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n)",

Target location must not contain box1\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_right\n    :parameters (?p - player ?from ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adj_right ?from ?to)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box box1 ?to))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_up\n    :parameters (?p - player ?from ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adj_up ?from ?to)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box box1 ?to))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action move_down\n    :parameters (?p - player ?from ?to - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adj_down ?from ?to)\n      (not (is_wall ?to))\n      (not (at_box box1 ?to))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Player pushing box actions (parameters remain as they involve the box directly)\n  (:action push_left\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_right ?player_loc ?box_loc)    ; Player is to the right of the box, pushing it left\n      (adj_left ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)    ; Box moves left\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))        ; New box location must not be a wall\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))      ; New box location must not have another box\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)             ; Player moves to old box location\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)            ; Box moves to new location\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_right\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_left ?player_loc ?box_loc)     ; Player is to the left of the box, pushing it right\n      (adj_right ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)   ; Box moves right\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_up\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_down ?player_loc ?box_loc)     ; Player is below the box, pushing it up\n      (adj_up ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)      ; Box moves up\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  (:action push_down\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_loc ?box_loc ?new_box_loc - location)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_loc)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_loc)\n      (adj_up ?player_loc ?box_loc)       ; Player is above the box, pushing it down\n      (adj_down ?box_loc ?new_box_loc)    ; Box moves down\n      (not (is_wall ?new_box_loc))\n      (not (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc))\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_loc))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_loc)\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_loc))\n      (at_box ?b ?new_box_loc)\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n)",
